## **What's Trending on TikTok? — November 2026 Roundup**

Discover the hottest trends on TikTok this month.

### **Trend:** **btw it’s cool to do both** **— November 13, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** Two sides of your life, one permission slip. Open with “btw it’s cool to do both,” then show two totally contrasting sides of your life, as a video or a carousel. Think gym rat and couch potato, or spreadsheets and sparkles. Lowkey the most freeing trend of the week.

**Audio:** [Original audio](https://www.tiktok.com/music/original-sound-7620922998769421087)

### **Trend:** **Building something AI can never replace** **— November 13, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** Plot twist: the most-liked thing on your feed is the one thing a robot can’t fake. Creators are sharing footage of what AI can’t replace, like human connection, and it’s hitting right as everyone’s feeling a little skeptical about AI. Wholesome, a little pointed, and we’re here for it.

**Audio:** [What You Waiting For? (Remastered 2019)](https://www.tiktok.com/music/What-You-Waiting-For-Remastered-2019-6759664513240270850)

**Trend Recap:** The bait-and-switch is the whole point. Creators list the “essentials” they bring to the table, and the answers are gloriously delulu, like packing makeup for a job interview. Great for beauty, lifestyle, and any brand that can poke fun at its own must-haves.

### **Trend: Some people call me... — September 18, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** Some people call you by your name. Some people are about to call you for the best \[insert specialty\] in town. This wordplay format flips your nicknames into your pitch; start with what people usually call you, land on what they should be calling you _for_. Clean, clever, and it doubles as a CTA without ever sounding like one.

**Trend Recap:** Maximalism's back and it's bedazzled. Creators are covering everything, products, accessories, their own skin, that one random object on the desk, in rhinestones, gems, and charms. Beauty, fashion, hair, nails, lifestyle: this is your sign to go full whimsical chaos.

## **What's Trending on TikTok? — May 2026 Roundup**

Discover the hottest trends on TikTok this month.

### **Trend:** **Sorry, my hands are full** **— May 29, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** When your favorites won't fit in two hands. Creators are stacking up the products they refuse to put down, piling them into their arms as the on-screen text reads _"Sorry, my hands are full."_ A flex for product brands and the easiest way to position your lineup as the irreplaceable starter pack.

**Audio:** [Original audio](https://www.tiktok.com/music/original-sound-7607556252155710221)

### **Trend:** **Sound of water vs sound of…** **— May 29, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** Water? Silent. Coffee? Whole soundtrack. Creators are splitting the screen between plain water and their drink of choice, and the moment the Coffee gets stirred (or the iced matcha gets shaken, or the soda gets poured), a song kicks in. A built-in win for any beverage brand with a personality.

**Audio:** REALLY GET INTO ITTT – mbaby

### **Trend:** **Saying things in different tones** **— May 29, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** One phrase, infinite reads. Pick a line, slap it on the screen, then deliver it in every tone you can think of: supportive, disappointed, sarcastic, flirty. Works solo, works even better with the whole team, and the brands nailing it are sneaking in subtle product placements between takes.

**Audio:** Original audio

### **Trend: My top 5 horror movies — May 15, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** This one's giving gratitude journal, but make it content. Since we're deep into May, creators are using the format "May this month \[insert gratitude\]" as a simple, feel-good way to connect with their audience. Sweet, seasonal, and easy to make your own.

**Audio:** Original audio

### **Trend: A little birdie told me — May 15, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** Grab your hoodie, grab your BFF, and get ready. One person suits up as the "little birdie", hood up, hands poking out, while the other delivers the news. It's goofy, it's endearing, and it's super easy to make brand-relevant: "a little birdie told me about the new drop..." More than enough reason to raid the wardrobe closet.

**Audio:** [Non, je ne regrette rien - Edith Piaf](https://www.tiktok.com/music/Non-je-ne-regrette-rien-223528131368980480?lang=en)

### **Trend: Office pilates — May 15, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** The EGC community has cracked the code on the 2 PM slump. Creators are turning their office chairs, desks, and whatever else is within reach into makeshift pilates equipment for a mid-day mini workout, and the results are hilarious. Works for any brand with a team willing to commit to the bit

**Audio:** [Let's go - Jaden Bojsen](https://www.tiktok.com/music/LET'S-GO-7398286867525552144?lang=en)

### **Trend: My top 5 horror movies — May 01, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** List-style trends are having a moment, and this one is _chef's kiss_ for brands. Creators are framing their biggest pet peeves, fears, or painfully relatable scenarios as fake "horror movie titles", think worst-case-scenario storytelling with a comedic twist. Perfect for any niche.

**Audio:** [Original sound](https://www.tiktok.com/music/original-sound-7608527302834146056)

### **Trend: It's gonna be MAY — May 01, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** It's officially May 1st, which means one thing: the millennial internet is _activated._ Creators are lip-syncing to NSYNC's "It's Gonna Be Me," leaning hard into the way Justin Timberlake pronounces "me" which, as we all know, sounds exactly like "May." Seasonal, nostalgic, and extremely on time.

**Audio:** [It's Gonna Be Me – NSYNC](https://www.tiktok.com/music/It's-Gonna-Be-Me-It's-gonna-gonna-gonna-7216013212889417730?lang=en)

### **Trend: This looks so cool, I have to capture it — May 01, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** Kicking off with the on-screen text "This looks so cool, I have to capture it," this trend is a simple but satisfying format for showing off products, recs, or anything visually worth savoring. Clip by clip, it builds a vibe, and it's super easy to make your own.

**Audio:** [Original audio](https://www.tiktok.com/music/original-sound-7630260874699262723?lang=en)

## **What's trending on TikTok? — April 2026 Roundup**

Discover the hottest trends on TikTok this month.

### **Trend:** **Shake your money maker — April 17, 2026**

**Trend Recap:**"When someone asks me to shake my money maker..." then you show them exactly what pays the bills. Whether it's your brand, your 9-to-5, or your product line, this one's fun, playful, and works for literally any niche.

**Audio:** [Original audio - Adri Monteblays](https://www.tiktok.com/music/original-sound-7625015741045148447)

**Current # of Posts:** 261

### **Trend:** **\#bieberchella — April 17, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** Bieberchella has taken over the FYP. Use "Baby" by Justin Bieber and switch looks, products, or vibes with every beat drop. The transitions need to be tight, so each switch lands perfectly on cue. Grab your friends or go solo.

**Audio:** [Original audio - Lochi](https://www.tiktok.com/music/original-sound-7263225464389520130)

**Current # of Posts:** 105.4K

### **Trend:** **Top 5 horror movies — April 17, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** Not actually about horror movies. Film a 7-second b-roll style clip, add "My top 5 horror movies" as text on screen, then list five things you can't stand that are painfully relatable to your niche. The bait-and-switch is the whole point.

**Audio:** [Originalton - Iron Mindset](https://www.tiktok.com/music/Originalton-Iron-Mindset-7398582962192780064?lang=en)

**Current # of Posts:** 59.8K

### **Trend: Do you actually — April 03, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** Do you actually… or are you just saying that? Creators lip sync while adding hyper-specific on-screen text that calls out a very real experience. If it hits, it _really_ hits.

**Audio:** [Original audio - B.](https://www.tiktok.com/music/original-sound-7608527302834146056)

**Current # of Posts:** 555.4K

### **Trend: Our marketing girl is OOO today — April 03, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** We're seeing a shift on how brands need to post online. Audiences want personality. They want to see real people behind the brand. And this trend delivers exactly that. Other team members take over the brand socials for the day, and the results are chaotic and comedic.

### **Trend: What's in my bag, POV — April 03, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** What’s in my bag, but POV edition. The camera goes _inside_ the bag while items are pulled out one by one. It’s playful, fast-paced, and a sneaky-good way to feature products.

## **What's trending on TikTok? — March 2026 Roundup**

Discover the hottest trends on TikTok this month.

### **Trend: Why you so obsessed with me — March 20, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** Why are you so obsessed… with this trend? Creators are stopping people mid-walk to get them to look, snapping a screenshot, then pairing it with the iconic sound like everyone’s staring at them. Delusional? A little. Entertaining? Very.

**Audio:** [Mariah Carey - Obsessed](https://www.tiktok.com/music/Obsessed-6917768908850071553)

**Current # of Posts:** 77K

### **Trend: iPhone transition — March 20, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** The iPhone understood the assignment. Start unready, swipe your phone across the screen, then reveal the glow-up through the camera lens while snapping pics with the volume button. The transition is smooth, cinematic, and weirdly satisfying.

**Audio:** [djreyz - Tout Donner X Selfie Pulla](https://www.tiktok.com/music/Tout-Donner-X-Selfie-Pulla-7571875366051793665)

**Current # of Posts:** 22.7K

### **Trend: 212 — March 20, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** 212 is back and still that girl. Just dance, add a bold or relatable text overlay, and let the attitude do the work. Simple format, main character energy.

**Audio:** [pusiquita - Azealia Banks . 212 instrumental](https://www.tiktok.com/music/Azealia-Banks-212-instrumental-7247272522982951686)

**Current # of Posts:** 148.2K

### **Trend: CBK — March 06, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** CBK summer is officially here. Inspired by the iconic love story of John F. Kennedy Jr. and Carolyn Bessette-Kennedy, creators are channeling effortless 90s minimalism with chic outfits, clean aesthetics, and “old money” vibes. Think quiet luxury, polished neutrals, and main character energy.

**Audio:** [american wedding](https://www.tiktok.com/music/american-wedding-7490585948888238871)

**Current # of Posts:** 213.1K

### **Trend: Alter ego — March 06, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** Two people, one iconic walk. This trend starts with one creator strutting and lip-syncing, then pans to their partner delivering Jennie’s part, like the ultimate duo reveal. The vibe? Luxury, date night, and walking like you own the street.

**Audio:** [Dracula (JENNIE Remix) - JENNIE&Tame Impala](https://www.tiktok.com/music/Dracula-JENNIE-Remix-7603221525400455169)

**Current # of Posts:** 56.2K

### **Trend: Transition dab — March 06, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** Dab and suddenly… glow up. This transition is all about instant before-and-after transformations using a quick movement synced to the sound. Perfect for outfit changes, location reveals, or showing how your product completely changes the vibe.

**Audio:** [original sound - dj bux](https://www.tiktok.com/music/original-sound-7580526049614285599)

**Current # of Posts:** 81.6K

## **What's trending on TikTok? — February 2026 Roundup**

Discover the hottest trends on TikTok this month.

### **Trend: You can learn someone’s character from their childhood photos — February 20, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** You really can learn everything from childhood photos. Start with a present-day clip, then hard cut to the baby pic for an instant “ohhhh that tracks” moment. It’s wholesome, slightly chaotic, and guaranteed to boost connection.

**Audio:** [Diva - Beyoncé](https://www.tiktok.com/music/Diva-6733074442043293698?lang=en)

**Current # of Posts:** 628.2K

### **Trend: Does he? — February 20, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** Does he do this?” is the new bare minimum check. Creators are calling out the little things, then cutting to “I do.” It’s playful, a little petty, and perfect for showing how you go above and beyond for clients.

**Audio:** [hey daddy - Usher Raymond](https://www.tiktok.com/music/hey-daddy-6432077887372790530?lang=en)

**Current # of Posts:** 951K

### **Trend: Googled my symptoms — February 20, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** Googled my symptoms and turns out… I just needed this. Add the on-screen text, build the suspense, then reveal your product, service, or painfully relatable solution. Dramatic? Yes. Effective? Also yes.

**Audio:** [Sally when the wine runs out - tucker](https://www.tiktok.com/music/sally-when-the-wine-runs-out-7465380137857534762?lang=en)

**Current # of Posts:** 168.1K

### **Trend: It's always warm right here — February 6, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** Wow, did the screen just get blurry? Cuddle in close with your friends, sweetheart, kids, or fur babies and prepare your followers for the warmest of fuzzies.

**Audio:** [Original sound - Joh Cooper](https://www.tiktok.com/music/original-sound-7448136937753676549)

**Current # of Posts:** 109.9K

### **Trend: Traitors — February 6, 2026**

**Trend Recap:** Faithful or traitor? Inspired by the hit show _Traitors_, here's a trend to celebrate your gossip buddies at work and (playfully, we hope) call out the others.

**Audio:** [Original sound - The Tab](https://www.tiktok.com/music/original-sound-7319939295929076512)

**Current # of Posts:** 1.7K
